### [38] [When Context Misleads: In-context Learning with Jurisdiction in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27603)
*Pei-lin Li,Qingle Liu,Junyang Feng,Siyu Li,Sunqi Fan,Xin-Sheng Chen,Shuojin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出ICL后训练方法忽视了"上下文权威性"判断能力，提出FakeContextBench基准，并发现微调反而增加被误导风险；为此提出J-ICL框架，将上下文验证纳入训练目标，使ICL能力与抗欺骗能力可同时提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL后训练方法擅长从示例中提取模式，却忽视了上下文权威性判断——即能否决定上下文信息是否应主导最终答案，导致模型易被误导性上下文欺骗，且缺乏相应评估基准。

Method: 提出FakeContextBench基准（涵盖七个领域的伪科学主张）用于评测；并提出Jurisdiction In-Context Learning (J-ICL)后训练框架，将上下文验证融入训练目标。

Result: 大规模预训练不足以实现可靠的上下文权威辨别；主流ICL微调方法使现实准确率相对基础模型最多下降14.95个百分点。J-ICL在四个骨干模型上平均提升ICLEval 5.84个百分点、现实准确率9.20个百分点，相对MetaICL与Symbol Tuning平均提升现实率18.09个百分点。

Conclusion: ICL能力与抵御欺骗性上下文的能力可以协同提升，J-ICL验证了这一可能性；基准已开源。

Abstract: In-Context Learning (ICL) has become a cornerstone of modern LLM deployment. However, existing ICL post-training methods have a critical blind spot: they excel at extracting patterns from demonstrations while often neglecting context authority, the ability to determine whether contextual information should govern the final answer. To benchmark this capability, we introduce FakeContextBench, which contains pseudoscientific claims across seven domains. Our evaluation of commercial and open-source models shows that large-scale pre-training alone is insufficient for reliable context-authority discrimination. Moreover, prevalent ICL fine-tuning methods can increase susceptibility to misleading context, reducing reality accuracy by up to 14.95 percentage points relative to the base model. To address this trade-off, we propose Jurisdiction In-Context Learning (J-ICL), a post-training framework that incorporates context validation into the training objective. Across four model backbones, J-ICL improves ICLEval by an average of 5.84 percentage points and reality accuracy by 9.20 points over the corresponding base models. It also raises the Reality Rate by an average of 18.09 points relative to MetaICL and Symbol Tuning. These results demonstrate that ICL capability and resistance to deceptive context can be improved together. The benchmark is available at https://github.com/peilin717/FakeContext-Bench.

</details>


### [39] [Can Jev Judge Radiology Reports? Evaluating a System One Model for Clinical Factuality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27607)
*Jiaju Huang,Hao Yang,Xinyu Ma,Xinglong Liang,Kunyan Cai,Junqiang Ma,Shaobin Chen,Yue Sun,Tao Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种低成本评估器 Jev，用于衡量 AI 生成放射报告与医生报告的事实差异（遗漏、添加、否定反转），并验证其与专家判断的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 生成的放射报告可能看似相似却遗漏、添加或反转异常，需要衡量这些事实差异以评估报告生成器。

Method: 将评估建模为 System One 决策模型，逐条判断每个陈述是否被另一报告支持，并双向组合以捕捉无支持声明与遗漏；同时与开放 NLI 评估器在相同分解与聚合下对比。

Result: 单问题配置在 RadEvalX 与 RadEvalExpert 上 Kendall 相关系数分别为 0.573 和 0.398，优于 NLI 评估器；每题一个支持问题可保持相近专家一致性并减少 43-45% 判断输入 token，每百对报告成本低于三美分；受控错误测试中假否定检测 AUROC 达 0.977；RadMatch 在临床显著错误上表现更强。

Conclusion: Jev 可作为测量生成放射报告事实差异的实用判断组件，同时指出基准一致性受报告大小与错误定义影响，复杂评估仍有价值。

Abstract: An AI-generated radiology report can resemble a physician's report while omitting an abnormality, adding an unsupported finding, or reversing its presence. Measuring these factual differences is essential for evaluating report generators. We study Jev, a System One decision model, as a simple, low-cost judge of agreement with physician-written reference reports. Our evaluator checks whether each statement is supported by the other report and combines these judgments in both directions to capture unsupported claims and omissions. A single-question configuration reaches Kendall correlations of 0.573 on RadEvalX and 0.398 on RadEvalExpert with expert error counts, outperforming an open natural language inference judge under matched decomposition and aggregation. One support question per statement retains similar expert agreement to seven while using 43-45% fewer judgment input tokens. At the documented API price, judgments cost under three cents per hundred report pairs, excluding local decomposition. In a separate controlled-error test, Jev detects false negation with an AUROC of 0.977. Local RadMatch achieves stronger agreement on clinically significant errors in both expert datasets and on total errors in the shared RadEvalExpert subset. Finding-count and error-scope analyses show that benchmark agreement reflects report size and error definitions as well as medical error detection. These results support Jev as a practical judgment component for measuring factual differences in generated radiology reports and identify where more elaborate evaluation remains valuable.

</details>


### [40] [Brain-to-Language Decoding: Tasks, Signals, Methods, Evaluation, Practical Use and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27650)
*Yiqian Yang,Yiqun Duan,Chenyu Liu,Yiqi Wang,Xinliang Zhou,Chin-Teng Lin,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述"脑到语言解码"领域，涵盖侵入式与非侵入式测量、三类任务（发音、内语、感知）及从识别到文本生成、个性化语音与面部动画的进展，并提出五级发展轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑到语言解码既能帮助失语者恢复交流，也是研究大脑如何表征语言的手段；神经记录与表征学习的进步亟需系统梳理该领域从受限识别到多样化输出的发展。

Method: 采用无起始年份限制的文献检索并更新至2026年9月，综合侵入式与非侵入式研究，将发音（Articulated）、内语（Inner）、感知（Perceived）任务与其涉及的神经群体、可用表征及可支持输出相连接，并比较模型、公共资源、评测演变及通信成本。

Result: 研究发现多条互补路径：语音、声学与语义目标各保留信息的不同侧面；共享表征支持跨记录条件与任务复用；在线交流依赖校准、反馈与用户控制；共享基准利于算法比较，纵向研究揭示持续使用的需求。

Conclusion: 论文总结现有进展与局限，并提出从指令、语言到意义、情境及双向认知交流的五级前瞻性发展轨迹。

Abstract: Brain-to-language decoding translates neural activity associated with language production, internal speech and perception into linguistic or expressive outputs. It offers a route to restoring communication after speech loss and a means of studying how the brain represents language. Advances in neural recording and representation learning have expanded the field from constrained recognition and acoustic reconstruction to text generation, streaming personalised speech and facial animation. This survey synthesises these developments across invasive and non-invasive measurements, drawing on a search without a lower year limit and source-led updates through September 2026. We connect Articulated, Inner and Perceived tasks to the neural populations they engage, the representations available to decoders and the outputs those representations can support. We examine model development, public resources and the evolution of evaluation, and compare published performance and communication costs within their reported protocols. The synthesis identifies complementary routes to progress: phonetic, acoustic and semantic targets preserve different aspects of a message; shared representations support reuse across recording conditions and tasks; and online communication increasingly depends on calibration, feedback and user control alongside decoding accuracy. Shared benchmarks enable algorithmic comparisons, while longitudinal studies reveal the demands of sustained use. We discuss these developments and their remaining limitations, then outline a prospective five-level trajectory from commands and language to meaning, scenarios and bidirectional cognitive exchange

</details>


### [41] [The Path Matters: Evaluating Small Language Models Beyond Answer Accuracy in KGQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27669)
*Eduin E. Hernandez,Sergio A. Diaz,Luis F. Garcia,Nurassyl Askar,Stefano Rini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将知识图谱问答中的导航能力独立出来，用THESEUS框架配合冻结的SLM作为动作策略进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端KGQA将图访问、搜索、导航、推理和答案生成耦合在一起，难以判断SLM是否能忠实执行问题隐含的推理路径，也难以将失败归因于导航而非其他阶段。

Method: 采用THESEUS导航与可追溯框架，使用冻结的现成SLM作为局部动作策略；每步环境给出合法出边动作，模型选择一个可执行动作并决定是否停止，不做任务特定参数更新、模型控制束搜索或自由文本生成。

Result: 在Kinship和MQuAKE-ST上，规模相近的本地模型在答案准确率和路径保真度上差异显著，两指标有时偏好不同模型；提示也有模型依赖效应，单一示例轨迹可能改善或损害导航。

Conclusion: 评估SLM图推理不应只看终点准确率，还需纳入路径保真度等轨迹指标。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are increasingly paired with knowledge graphs (KGs), yet end-to-end KG question answering conflates graph access, search, navigation, reasoning, and answer generation. This coupling makes it difficult both to determine whether an SLM can faithfully execute the reasoning path implied by a question and to attribute failures to navigation rather than to other stages of the pipeline. We isolate this capability by employing the THESEUS navigation and traceability framework and using frozen, off-the-shelf SLMs as local action policies. At each hop, the environment exposes the legal outgoing graph actions, and the model selects one executable graph action and decides whether to stop, without task-specific parameter updates, model-controlled beam search, or free-form answer generation. This controlled setting allows us to evaluate terminal-answer accuracy with Hits@1 together with path fidelity, using Path Edit Distance (PED) as the primary trajectory metric. Across the Kinship and MQuAKE-ST KGQAs, similarly sized local models differ substantially in answer accuracy and path fidelity, with the two metrics sometimes favoring different models. This model-dependent behavior also extends to prompting, as a single demonstrated trajectory can improve or degrade navigation depending on the model. These results motivate evaluating SLM graph reasoning beyond endpoint accuracy alone.

</details>


### [42] [Same Scores, Different Decisions: Evaluating JEV and Language Models for Legal Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27678)
*Fan Zhang,Yankai Chen,Zhuohan Xie,Yixi Zhou,Sijia Peng,Lei Fan,Xinhua Ji,Cunyuan Zheng,Huangyong Shan,Philip S. Yu,Xue Liu,Yu Chen,Preslav Nakov,Songwei He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了Jev与九种语言模型在ContractNLI上的表现，评估成本、响应时间与准确性，强调需超越聚合准确率、关注请求配置变化下的个体判断稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合同推理需要对共享文档做多项判断，但聚合准确率会掩盖个体决策变化；且反复一致也可能只是稳定地犯错，因此需要更细致的评估视角。

Method: 在ContractNLI上对比Jev与九个语言模型，评估推理成本、响应时间、平均正确率及重复请求下的正确性；控制假设可见性、请求输出与输出顺序，固定合同和目标判断。

Result: Jev成本最低、响应时间中位数最小，托管语言模型基线准确率更高；基线准确率排名与各条件/重复下正确性排名不同，但微小差异不足以证明普遍稳定性优势。诊断还发现补偿性纠错与退化及持续错误。

Conclusion: 评估合同推理模型应同时考虑成本与响应时间，以及请求配置变化时个体判断是否保持正确。代码已开源。

Abstract: Contract inference requires multiple judgments about a shared document, but aggregate accuracy can conceal changes in the individual decisions. Repeated agreement is also insufficient: a model may consistently return the wrong answer. In this paper, we compare Jev with nine language models on ContractNLI, evaluating inference cost, response time, average correctness, and correctness across repeated request conditions. Controlled comparisons vary hypothesis visibility, requested outputs, and output order while keeping the contract and target judgment fixed. Jev has the lowest cost and median response time among the evaluated configurations, while hosted language models achieve higher baseline accuracy. Rankings by baseline accuracy differ from rankings by correctness across every condition and repeat, although small differences in the latter do not establish a general stability advantage. Development diagnostics further reveal compensating corrections and regressions, as well as persistent errors. These findings motivate evaluating cost and response time alongside whether individual judgments remain correct as the request configuration changes. Code: https://github.com/ZF-Utokyo/Jev-Benchmark

</details>


### [43] [SkillGym: Internalizing Human Skills into LLMs for Real-World Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27717)
*Zhilong Ge,Yuting Shao,Yutao Yang,Yuxuan Cai,Jie Zhou,Kai Chen,Bo Zhang,Qin Chen,Liang He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillGym将人类编写的agent技能转化为可执行、可验证的训练环境，构建2756个环境并微调模型，使35B模型在多项基准上超越更强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类编写的agent技能蕴含丰富工作流程，但通常仅作为推理时的外部指令使用，未被内化为模型自身的可复用能力。

Method: 提出SkillGym：skill-to-task流水线实例化任务、用代码检查器验证结果、通过对比执行评估技能依赖；构建12类2756个环境，收集多模型8364条成功轨迹（平均49次工具调用、超60k token），用于监督微调和基于结果奖励的强化学习。

Result: 在Claude Code下，SFT使Qwen3.5-35B-A3B在GDPval-AA v2提升199 Elo、Terminal-Bench 2.1提升19.10个百分点，SkillsBench v1.1在有/无技能下分别提升28.13和12.38个百分点。35B SkillGym-Agent在技能辅助SkillsBench达51.47%，超过Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5.4 Mini和DeepSeek V4 Pro。无技能时也超越Codex和Claude Code下的技能辅助基线。

Conclusion: 将技能内化为可复用程序性能力是可行的，使较小模型无需外部技能也能具备竞争力。

Abstract: Human-written agent skills encode rich workflows for real-world problem solving, but are typically used as external inference-time instructions rather than internalized as reusable model capabilities. We introduce \texttt{SkillGym}, a framework that transforms these skills into executable, verifiable training environments for large language model agents. Its skill-to-task pipeline instantiates concrete tasks, verifies outcomes with code-based checkers, and assesses empirical skill dependence through contrastive executions. We construct and release 2,756 environments across 12 categories and collect 8,364 successful trajectories from multiple models and harnesses, averaging 49 tool calls and over 60k logged text tokens. These resources support supervised fine-tuning on verified workflows and reinforcement learning with outcome-based rewards. Under Claude Code, supervised fine-tuning improves Qwen3.5-35B-A3B by 199 Elo on GDPval-AA v2, 19.10 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.1, and 28.13 and 12.38 points on SkillsBench v1.1 with and without skills, respectively. Our 35B \texttt{SkillGym-Agent} reaches 51.47\% on skill-assisted SkillsBench, exceeding reported scores for Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5.4 Mini, and DeepSeek V4 Pro. Without skills, it also surpasses skill-assisted bases under Codex and Claude Code, suggesting reusable procedural competence.

</details>


### [44] [Beyond Unsafe Detection: Counterfactually Anchored Evidence Attribution for Multi-Turn LLM Safety Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27773)
*Srinivasan Subramanian,Kazi Aminul Islam,Md. Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出多轮对话中的护栏失败归因方法：构建含行为验证和分层证据监督的多轮数据集，训练轻量级层次化归因模型，同时检测安全违规并定位关键用户轮次和token片段，在检测和误报控制上显著优于关键词基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有护栏评估框架多只关注单轮结果（请求是否安全），无法应对对抗意图分散在多轮中的多轮失败场景，因此需要超越检测，识别将对话推向不安全轨迹的具体轮次和token。

Method: 构建含行为验证与分层证据监督的多轮数据集（1,762段对话，含对抗对话、良性孪生和高风险词汇良性变体）；训练轻量级层次化归因模型，预测安全违规并归因到贡献性用户轮次和token片段。

Result: 检测F1=0.988；移除归因最高的15% token使对抗分类置信度下降51.1%；在边界良性和高风险词汇良性对话上误报率均低于1%，而关键词表面风险基线分别为37.3%和94.7%；人工标注显示84.5%的对抗案例中，模型前五归因轮次包含人类认定的证据轮次。

Conclusion: 该工作将护栏评估从仅判断安全与否推进到对多轮失败进行轮次和token级别的证据归因，验证了模型检测与归因的有效性及低误报优势。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) move from conversational assistants to advanced agentic systems, guardrail failures can convert adversarial intents into harmful executions. However, most guardrail evaluation frameworks focus only on the result and assess whether a user request is safe or unsafe. This approach is insufficient for multi-turn failures, where adversarial intent is distributed across multiple turns. This motivates us to go beyond detection to identify the turns and tokens that push the conversation toward unsafe trajectories. To support this, we construct a multi-turn dataset with behavioral validation and tiered evidence supervision. The dataset contains 1,762 conversations, including adversarial conversations, benign twins, and benign variants with high-risk vocabulary. We train a lightweight hierarchical attribution model that predicts safety violations and attributes them to contributing user turns and token spans. The model achieves strong detection performance (F1=0.988), and removing the top 15% of attributed tokens reduces the adversarial classification confidence by 51.1%. The model preserves low false positive rates on benign conversations with high-risk vocabulary, with false positives below 1% on both borderline benign and benign high-risk vocabulary conversations, compared to 37.3% and 94.7% for a keyword-based surface-risk baseline. Independent human annotation supports the model's attribution performance, with the top-five attributed turns containing a human-identified evidence-bearing turn in 84.5% of adversarial cases.

</details>


### [45] [LabourCrew: A Multi-Agent RAG Framework for Trustworthy Adversarial Deliberation and Statutory Reasoning over Labour Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27814)
*Fatema Tuj Johora Faria,Mukaffi Bin Moin,Jubayer Al Mahmud,M. F. Mridha,Md. Alam Hossain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 LabourCrew 多智能体 RAG 框架，通过 StatuteGraph、证据交换协议与校准信任门三个机制，使劳动法问答的引用可溯源、可校验，在低资源法定问答中实现可审计的可靠作答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法定问答中每条主张都必须可溯源到证据，仅有相关性不够，因为不可验证的劳动权答案会带来严重法律后果。现有系统存在缺陷：单轮 RAG 无法检测证据不足，而多智能体法律辩论系统把 grounding 当成提示约定，允许智能体引用未被检索到的证据。

Method: 提出 LabourCrew 多智能体 RAG 框架，围绕三种 grounding 机制：(1) StatuteGraph，显式链接章、节、但书与交叉引用结构的图索引，而非固定长度文本片段；(2) Evidence Exchange Protocol，将辩护方与解释器限制在证据账本内，使引用未检索文本不可能发生，同时由容错监督委员会并行运行辩护方，使个体失败降级而非系统崩溃；(3) Calibrated Trust Gate，用信任分数替代分类式接受/拒绝，并通过 conformal risk control 设阈值，给假接受率提供无分布假设的界限。

Result: 在 LabourActQA（来自 2006 年孟加拉国劳动法、含 7 类推理与 3 个难度层级的 500 条孟加拉语问题集）上评估：假接受率降至 0.081，处于目标水平 α=0.10 之内；答案相关性在 HyDE RAG、Graph-RAG、Hierarchical RAG 中最高（0.862 > 0.839、0.815、0.828）；随问题难度上升表现为渐进退化而非灾难性崩溃。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在低资源法定领域，使法律问答可审计的关键是校准式弃答（calibrated abstention），而不仅是检索质量本身。该工作说明可溯源的证据机制与受控风险阈值是提升法律问答可靠性的核心。

Abstract: In statutory question answering, every claim must be traceable to evidence, not merely relevant, since unverifiable labour-rights answers carry serious legal consequences. Current systems fall short: single-pass RAG cannot detect insufficient evidence, while multi-agent legal-debate systems treat grounding as a prompting convention, letting agents cite unretrieved evidence. To address this gap, we introduce LabourCrew, a multi-agent RAG framework built around three grounding mechanisms: StatuteGraph, a graph index that explicitly links chapter, section, proviso, and cross-reference structure rather than fixed-length spans; an Evidence Exchange Protocol that confines advocates and an interpreter to an evidence ledger, making citation to unretrieved text impossible, while a fault-tolerant supervisor board runs advocates in parallel so individual failures degrade rather than crash the system; and a Calibrated Trust Gate that replaces categorical accept/reject decisions with a trust score, thresholded via conformal risk control for a distribution-free bound on the false-accept rate. We evaluate on LabourActQA, a 500-item Bangla question set from the Bangladesh Labour Act, 2006, spanning seven reasoning categories and three difficulty tiers. The framework drives the empirical false-accept rate to 0.081, within the target level ($α= 0.10$), achieves the highest Answer Relevancy among HyDE RAG, Graph-RAG, and Hierarchical RAG (0.862 $>$ 0.839, 0.815, 0.828), and degrades gradually rather than catastrophically as question difficulty increases. These results show that calibrated abstention, not retrieval quality alone, is what makes legal question answering auditable in low-resource statutory domains.

</details>


### [46] [Delegated Misalignment: How Multi-Agent Structures Amplify LLM Safety Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27900)
*Zonghao Ying,Jiaqi Yan,Huize Luo,Quanchen Zou,Aishan Liu,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示单个LLM的安全对齐无法迁移到多智能体场景，提出"委派失配"现象及其两大机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多部署于多智能体系统，但安全对齐几乎只按单智能体威胁模型评估，把安全当作单个模型的属性。作者要检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 提出"委派失配"(delegated misalignment)概念，用三条件协议在6个前沿LLM、49个危险任务上测试，并进行消融实验。

Result: 委派显著放大端到端危害：DeepSeek-V3.2完全执行率从30.6%升至77.6%；GPT-5单智能体22.5% vs 从属角色61.2%。单层标准防御各自失效甚至适得其反。

Conclusion: 安全对齐需超越"逐模型对齐"，转向面向多智能体系统的复合安全机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-agent systems where a principal agent decomposes tasks and delegates them to subordinate agents that may invoke external tools. Safety alignment, however, is still evaluated almost exclusively under a single-agent threat model, treating safety as a property of the individual LLM. We show that this assumption breaks down: \emph{individual safety alignment fails to transfer to multi-agent settings}. Two failure mechanisms emerge under delegation: \emph{responsibility diffusion} on the principal side and \emph{role-bias compliance} on the subordinate side, jointly converting language-level refusal into actionable harm. We refer to this phenomenon as \textit{delegated misalignment} and study it through a three-condition protocol across 6 frontier LLMs on 49 hazardous tasks. Delegation amplifies end-to-end harm substantially: DeepSeek-V3.2's full-execution rate rises from 30.6\% to 77.6\% once delegation is introduced, and the same model behaves very differently across roles (GPT-5: 22.5\% as a single agent vs.\ 61.2\% as a subordinate). Ablations further show that standard single-layer defenses each fail on their own and can even backfire. We call on the community to move beyond per-model alignment and toward composite safety mechanisms before multi-agent LLM systems are deployed at scale.

</details>


### [47] [Learning When Not to Listen: Selective Anti-Interference Pretraining for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27925)
*Jinchang Zhu,Haowei He,Yi Ding,Rong Fu,Nie Xiaojian,Shuangyong Song,Zhongjiang He,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出SPAR预训练目标，通过选择性抗干扰正则化让语言模型对无关远距离前缀不敏感，提升长上下文鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型会过度依赖无关的前置文本：即使局部上下文已足够支持预测，遥远且无关的前缀扰动仍会改变预测结果。这种干扰在长上下文、packed序列或含大量干扰项的语境中尤为严重。

Method: SPAR运行原始序列与仅改动远前缀的corrupt-prefix输入，利用短上下文充分性门控（基于模型估计远前缀是否提供额外信息）和门控KL目标，稳定局部已充分支持的suffix预测。

Result: 机制分析显示门控能识别局部充分token并显著降低其对前缀的敏感性。在Qwen2.5-0.5B/3B、Llama-3.2-1B、Llama-3.1-8B、GPT2-XL上等计算量的继续训练中均提升RULER；预训练实验在RULER和NoLiMa上也有增益。

Conclusion: 选择性抗干扰是一种有效的目标层面信号，可提升模型对上下文的鲁棒使用。

Abstract: Language models can over-condition on irrelevant preceding text: predictions already supported by local context may still change when distant, unrelated prefix tokens are perturbed. This interference is especially consequential in long, packed, or distractor-heavy contexts, where useful evidence and irrelevant spans coexist. We propose Selective Prefix Anti-Interference Regularization (SPAR), a pretraining objective for selective anti-interference. SPAR runs the original sequence and a corrupt-prefix input in which only the far prefix is changed, then uses a short-context sufficiency gate and a gated KL objective to stabilize locally supported suffix predictions. The gate operationalizes a model-based estimate of whether the far prefix supplies additional information about the target token. Mechanism analyses show that the gate identifies locally sufficient tokens and sharply reduces prefix sensitivity on gate-selected suffix tokens. In continued training on pretrained base models, SPAR improves RULER across Qwen2.5-0.5B, Qwen2.5-3B, Llama-3.2-1B, Llama-3.1-8B, and GPT2-XL under equal counted training compute; pretraining experiments further show gains on both RULER and NoLiMa. These results show that selective anti-interference is an effective objective-level signal for robust context use.

</details>


### [48] [From Sentiment Classification to Actionable and Responsible Feedback: A Scoping Review and Evidence Map of NLP in Student Evaluation of Teaching, 2015-2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27939)
*Jeff Eicher,Rafael da Silva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这是一篇PRISMA-ScR范围综述，映射2015–2026年间421篇将NLP应用于学生评教（SET）开放评语的研究。核心发现是"可行动性断层"：达到输出级证据(A2+)的研究占61.3%，而达到预期使用者评估级(A3+)的仅占11.6%，下降49.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP在SET中的技术路线从词典、传统分类器演进到Transformer和LLM，但技术多样化是否带来教育价值与证据稳健性的同步提升尚不明确，本文旨在系统检验这一落差。

Method: 采用PRISMA-ScR范围综述，筛选421篇研究（2015–2026，2026部分收录），沿一条技术轴(RQ1)与四个价值维度(RQ2–RQ5)进行编码。使用双盲LLM筛选加抽样人工裁定，覆盖七个抽取域，并在综合分析阶段对编码手册边界进行针对性复核。

Result: 最显著的量化缺口是"可行动性不连续"：输出级或更强(A2+)258篇(61.3%)，而预期使用者评估级或更强(A3+)仅49篇(11.6%)，落差49.7个百分点。情感分析仍是最主流任务(300/421)；诊断与生成深度(D4–D5)仅占已判定案例的28.2%；正式公平性指标极为罕见(1.9%)。

Conclusion: 结论为描述性，不支持"技术进步即教育价值进步"的因果主张。技术并存与报告不均衡是图谱的一部分，而A2+到A3+的断崖才是核心贡献，而非质量阶梯。

Abstract: Natural language processing (NLP) applied to open-ended teaching-evaluation comments (Student Evaluation of Teaching, SET) has tracked the field's technical evolution--from lexicons and conventional classifiers to transformers and large language models (LLMs)--but it is not evident that this technical diversification has been accompanied by corresponding gains in educational value and robustness of the evidence. This scoping review (PRISMA-ScR) maps 421 studies (2015-2026, 2026 partial) along a technical axis (RQ1) and four value dimensions (RQ2-RQ5). Dual mutually blinded LLM screening with sampled human adjudication coded seven extraction domains, with targeted codebook-boundary review at synthesis. The joint map's sharpest quantified gap is the actionability discontinuity: demonstrated output or stronger (A2+: 258/421; 61.3%) versus intended-user evaluation or stronger (A3+: 49/421; 11.6%), a 49.7 percentage-point drop. Sentiment analysis remains the modal task (300/421); diagnostic and generative depth is a substantial minority (D4-D5: 28.2% of resolved cases); a formal fairness metric is rare (1.9%). The findings are descriptive and do not support causal claims of progress: technological coexistence and uneven reporting are part of the map, but the A2+ to A3+ cliff is the contribution, not a quality ladder.

</details>


### [49] [Six Layers Less: Encoder Pruning for Whisper with Label-Free Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27980)
*Rasmus Aagaard,Nicki Skafte Detlefsen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种Whisper编码器层剪枝方法，通过逐层剔除(WER变化最小)移除6层(18.5%)，无需自定义推理代码，并通过无标签单语语音蒸馏恢复性能，四语言平均WER从21.9%降至20.1%（基线18.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前剪枝集中在Whisper解码器（如turbo将解码器从32层减到4层），编码器压缩虽被尝试但未获广泛采用，原因是压缩模型通常需要自定义推理实现，难以推广。

Method: 采用leave-one-layer-out策略，按移除单层后WER变化对编码器各层排序，移除影响最小的6层；随后使用无标签单语语音数据进行蒸馏以恢复零样本剪枝造成的性能损失。剪枝后模型仍是普通浅层编码器，无需自定义推理代码。

Result: 移除6层（占编码器18.5%）。零样本四语言平均WER为21.9%，蒸馏后降至20.1%，基线为18.2%。代码与模型均已开源发布。

Conclusion: 通过对Whisper编码器进行免自定义实现的层剪枝并结合蒸馏，可在保留标准推理流程的同时有效压缩编码器，减少性能损失，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Pruning large pre-trained transformer-based ASR models such as OpenAI's Whisper has seen great adoption, as pruning the decoder led to significant end-to-end transcription speedups. For instance, the {\tt whisper-large-v3-turbo} variant reduced the decoder from 32 to 4 layers, while Distill-Whisper similarly reduced the decoder to only 2 layers. Although some attention has been put towards reducing the size of the encoder, no approach has seen wide adoption. This could be due to the need for custom inference implementations to take advantage of the compressed model. We present an approach that ranks encoder layers by the leave-one-layer-out change in Word Error Rate (WER). The six layers that cause the least change are removed, corresponding to $18.5\%$ of the encoder stack. The pruned model requires no custom inference code as it is simply a more shallow encoder with fewer layers. We further distill using unlabeled monolingual speech data to recover performance degradation caused by the zero-shot layer pruning. Mean WER across four languages increases to $20.1\%$ after distillation, compared to $21.9\%$ zero-shot, going from a baseline of $18.2\%$. We release all of our code (https://github.com/rasgaard/whisper-encoder-layer-prune) and the pruned model (https://huggingface.co/rasgaard/whisper-large-v3-turbo-encoder-pruned).

</details>


### [50] [Risk-Controlled KV-Cache Eviction: From Memory Budgets to Risk Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27981)
*Beomgu Kang,SoJin Yun,Hojoon Kim,Hyunseok Seo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将KV-cache逐出重构为部署风险控制问题，用有限样本认证程序在给定可靠性契约下选出保留策略，否则回退全KV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均质量-内存权衡会掩盖部分请求效用严重退化的情况，需要针对单请求退化率的部署级风险控制。

Method: 定义相对全KV推理、超出容差的退化事件，以部署风险为其总体频率；基于校准数据，用与压缩器无关的事后认证程序，在目标风险与置信度约束下选择保留策略，无认证策略时回退全KV。

Result: 在多种逐出方法、Llama/Mistral、LongBench与RULER-32K上，同一契约支持不同逐出水平：Llama LongBench认证SnapKV 75%保留，RULER-32K无压缩策略通过而回退；经验退化率低于5%的策略仍可能认证失败；Llama LongBench上经验阈值法选了未认证策略，比固定预算方法少保留5-10个百分点缓存。

Conclusion: 该框架把部署级可靠性要求转化为KV内存运行点，避免仅凭经验退化率导致的过度压缩风险。

Abstract: KV-cache eviction is typically evaluated through average quality-memory trade-offs, yet a small average loss can hide requests whose utility degrades materially. We reformulate eviction as a deployment risk-control problem: a material degradation occurs when eviction lowers task utility by more than a deployment-specified tolerance relative to full-KV inference on the same request, and deployment risk is the population frequency of such events. Given a reliability contract specifying a target risk level and confidence requirement, we use a compressor-agnostic post-hoc certification procedure to select a retention policy from calibration data with a finite-sample guarantee, falling back to full KV when no compressed policy is certified. Across multiple eviction methods, Llama and Mistral models, and LongBench and RULER-32K, the same contract supports substantially different levels of eviction: on Llama, it certifies SnapKV at 75% retention on LongBench but no tested compressed policy on RULER-32K, triggering full-KV fallback. Policies with empirical degradation rates below the 5% target can still fail finite-sample certification; on Llama LongBench, empirical thresholding selects uncertified policies that retain 5-10 percentage points less cache across fixed-budget methods. The proposed framework converts a deployment-level reliability requirement into a KV-memory operating point.

</details>


### [51] [Controlled Attribute-Specific Summarization of Interrogative Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28004)
*A Aditya Bhardwaj,Arjit Singh Arora,Md Shad Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CASPER框架，用属性特定思维链提示和迭代精炼生成讯问对话摘要，并构建MINDSum数据集，在事实一致性和语义指标上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审讯/取证对话摘要需高事实准确性、连贯性与属性相关性，但现有摘要模型难以满足高风险领域要求。

Method: CASPER结合结构化提示与迭代精炼；构建MINDSum数据集（6000对语句，标注事件、事实、人物描述与填充语）；采用RoleEval分层评估（officer/inspector/senior inspector多角色按标准迭代评估），融合实体抽取与结构化反馈循环。

Result: 在ROUGE词法与BERTScore语义指标上优于标准摘要模型，且在事实一致性与上下文完整性上显著提升，人类评估证实其与专家推理一致。

Conclusion: 受控摘要在高风险领域具有潜力，为AI驱动的取证情报提供方向。

Abstract: Effective summarization of interrogative dialogues is a critical task in forensic and investigative settings, requiring high factual accuracy, coherence, and attribute-specific relevance. In this work, we introduce CASPER, a novel Chain-of-Thought Attribute-Specific Prompting for Evaluative Summarization framework that leverages structured prompting and iterative refinement to generate high-quality summaries of interrogator-witness interactions. We construct MINDSum, a dataset extending the MIND corpus, comprising 6,000 utterance pairs annotated with event details, factual statements, character descriptions, and fillers. CASPER employs RoleEval, a hierarchical evaluation mechanism where multiple roles (officer, inspector, senior inspector) iteratively assess summaries based on predefined criteria. By integrating entity extraction and structured feedback loops, CASPER significantly improves factual consistency and contextual completeness compared to existing baselines. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework outperforms standard summarization models on both lexical (ROUGE) and semantic (BERTScore) metrics, while human evaluation confirms its alignment with expert reasoning. Our findings underscore the potential of controlled summarization in high-stakes domains, paving the way for AI-driven forensic intelligence.

</details>


### [52] [Evaluating Open-Weight LLMs for Turkish Domain Documents Under Retrieval and Hardware Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28007)
*Imtiaz Ul Hassan,Öykü Akbulut,Onur Kaya,Ardhendu Behera,Swagat Kumar,Peter Matthew,Yonghuai Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文在资源受限的本地硬件上评估五个7B-8B开源模型对土耳其语领域文档的问答能力，提出一种将检索失败与推理失败分离的评价协议，发现端到端准确率49%-75%，且检索配置未显著优于字符TF-IDF基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有土耳其语大模型多采用通用基准测试，缺乏对长篇、结构复杂领域文档的评估，且忽略了资源受限本地部署场景下检索、推理与硬件约束的联合影响。

Method: 使用一份109页工业研发报告构建100个系统验证的问题作为主基准，并用112页公共部门报告的100题集复现；在6GB显存的NVIDIA RTX 3050上以4位量化、受控提示与解码方式本地评估五个7B-8B开源模型；提出带证据标注的评价协议以区分检索失败与下游推理失败；比较7种词法、稠密与混合检索配置，采用95% Wilson区间和精确配对McNemar检验。

Result: 主基准端到端准确率为49%-75%；所有检索配置在两个文档上均未显著优于字符TF-IDF基线；证据召回率在两份报告中饱和程度不同，表明检索与有效上下文容量对部分文档构成约束瓶颈。

Conclusion: 在土耳其语领域文档的开放权重模型部署中，模型选择、检索行为与硬件限制必须分别评估，检索能力可能成为部分文档的决定性瓶颈。

Abstract: Most Turkish-capable large language models (LLMs) are evaluated using general-purpose benchmarks rather than long, structurally complex domain documents. This paper evaluates five open-weight 7B-8B models for Turkish document question answering under a resource-constrained local deployment setting. The primary benchmark contains 100 systematically validated questions derived from a 109-page industrial R&D report, and the evaluation protocol is replicated using a second 112-page public-sector report and an independently constructed 100-question set. All models are evaluated locally on an NVIDIA RTX 3050 laptop GPU with 6 GB VRAM using controlled prompting, decoding, and 4-bit quantisation.
  The principal methodological contribution is an evidence-annotated evaluation protocol that separates retrieval failure from downstream model reasoning failure without requiring additional model calls. On the primary benchmark, end-to-end accuracy ranges from 49% to 75%. Seven lexical, dense, and hybrid retrieval configurations are additionally compared using 95% Wilson intervals and exact paired McNemar tests; none significantly outperforms the character TF-IDF baseline on either document. Evidence recall saturates differently across the two reports, showing that retrieval and effective context capacity can be binding constraints for some documents but not others. These results demonstrate that model selection, retrieval behaviour, and hardware limits must be evaluated separately when deploying open-weight LLMs for Turkish domain documents.

</details>


### [53] [Evaluating Feedback Focus and Pedagogical Adaptivity in LLM-Generated Feedback on Student Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28026)
*Norah Almousa,Shayan Peyghambari Oskoui,Raquel Coelho,Gayle Rogers,Xiang Lorraine Li,Diane Litman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FeedType基准，用Narciss分类法细化为7种反馈聚焦类型，标注了三门大学写作课程中教师与6个LLM（3种提示策略）的反馈，发现LLM虽覆盖多数类型，但分布与教师不符，适应性也无一匹配教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前评估多关注反馈特征、学习效果及反馈对象，但反馈的"聚焦"（focus）与"适应性"（adaptivity）被忽视，缺乏衡量LLM反馈是否符合专家教师教学实践的基准。

Method: 采纳并细化Narciss分类法为7种反馈聚焦类型，对教师和LLM生成的反馈进行标注；构建FeedType基准，涵盖6个LLM在3种提示策略下的反馈；评估反馈聚焦类型的覆盖度与分布，并检验LLM是否像专家教师一样针对不同草稿阶段和学生表现水平调整反馈。

Result: 多数LLM覆盖大多数反馈聚焦类型，但其分布无法反映教师反馈的分布；LLM表现出不同程度的适应性，但没有一个能匹配教师的适应性行为。

Conclusion: LLM生成的反馈在聚焦分布与适应性上与专家教师存在差距，FeedType基准可支持未来关于LLM反馈生成教学对齐性的研究。

Abstract: We investigate whether state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) generate feedback that reflects the pedagogical practices of expert teachers in terms of feedback focus and adaptivity. Previous evaluation efforts have examined feedback characteristics, its impact on learning, and its target, yet the focus of feedback and its adaptivity remains largely overlooked. To bridge this gap, we adopt and refine Narciss's taxonomy into seven feedback focus types to annotate teacher and LLM-generated feedback across three university writing courses. We release FeedType, a benchmark containing annotated teacher and LLM feedback from six LLMs under three prompting strategies. We assess the coverage and distribution of feedback focus types, and examine whether LLMs adapt their feedback across draft stages and student performance levels as an expert instructor does. Our findings show that while most LLMs cover most feedback focus types, they fail to reflect teacher feedback distributions and show varying levels of adaptivity, with none matching the teachers' adaptive behavior. We believe FeedType will support future research on pedagogical alignment in LLM feedback generation.

</details>


### [54] [How Much Were You Told? Measuring External Information in Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28041)
*Matthieu Dubois,Pablo Piantanida,François Yvon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Self-Conditioning，一种无监督信息论估计器，通过测量评审中"外部信息"量区分完全委托LLM生成与仅机器润色的评审，AUC可达1.0，且对表面改写不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会议政策区分"用LLM润色自己的评审"与"完全委托LLM进行批判"，但现有ARTIFICIAL TEXT DETECTION方法主要测量表面形式而非内容来源，无法有效区分。

Method: Self-Conditioning是一种无监督信息论估计器，测量评审携带的外部信息（即无法由被评审论文和通用评审指令解释的信息）。它比较评审在"生产上下文"下的似然，与上下文被评审自身提取的提示增强后的似然。

Result: 在IntelLabs同行评审基准上，Self-Conditioning以最高1.0的AUC区分完全委托与机器润色评审，同时对表面改写基本不敏感。随生成器获得的外部信息增多，其得分单调趋近人类区间，优于标准ATD基线。高温采样可规避该估计器，但会牺牲输出质量。

Conclusion: 测量外部信息比表面形式更能反映评审内容的来源，Self-Conditioning为区分LLM润色与委托评审提供了有效且鲁棒的方案。

Abstract: Conference policies distinguish using Large Language Models (LLMs) to polish one's own review from delegating the critique, but current Artificial Text Detection (ATD) methods largely measure surface form rather than the origin of its content. We instead measure the external information carried by a review: information not explained by the reviewed paper and a generic reviewing instruction. We propose Self-Conditioning, an unsupervised information-theoretic estimator that compares the likelihood of a review under its production context with its likelihood when that context is augmented with hints extracted from the review itself. On the IntelLabs peer-review benchmark, Self-Conditioning separates fully-delegated from machine-polished reviews with AUC up to $1.0$ while remaining largely insensitive to surface rewriting. Moreover, as generators receive increasing amounts of externally-provided information, their scores move monotonically towards the human regime, unlike standard ATD baselines. High-temperature sampling can evade the estimator, but at the cost of output quality.

</details>


### [55] [TEMPS: Temporal Sentence Embeddings for Temporal Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28048)
*Mourad Hassani,Julien Romero,Amel Bouzeghoub,Christian Jacquelinet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出时间文本相似度(TTS)任务与TEMPS模型，通过将时间表达式锚定为区间并用高斯建模监督时间编码器，补充语义检索的时间维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息检索系统很少表示时间，但在临床、新闻、法律等场景中，事件发生时间决定文档相关性。密集检索与RAG管道在主题匹配上表现好，却在时间上表现差，会返回主题相关但时间错误的内容。

Method: 定义TTS任务衡量两个锚定文本在时间上的对齐程度。提出TEMPS——一个可挂载到冻结语义检索器的模块化时间分支：将锚定时间表达式解析为区间，与每个区间进行"moment-match"映射到高斯分布；由此产生的排序监督一个锚定日期条件编码器，推理时将其得分与语义得分融合。训练用grounding自动生成监督，无需人工标注时间数据。时间得分基于分布嵌入的高斯-KL包含度量。

Result: 在三个时间基准上，TEMPS对每个测试的语义骨干模型都提升了MRR；在TS-Retriever上，R@1从先前时间SOTA的19.92提升到25.39。

Conclusion: TEMPS通过grounding与moment-matched监督为语义检索补充时间信号，无需人工标注，可即插即用地提升多种骨干模型的时序检索性能。

Abstract: Modern information retrieval (IR) systems rarely represent time, yet many information needs depend on it: in clinical, journalistic, and legal search, when an event occurred can decide whether a document is relevant. Dense retrievers and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines match queries to documents well on topic but poorly on time, so they surface content that is on-topic yet temporally wrong. We introduce Temporal Textual Similarity (TTS), a task that measures how well two anchored texts align in time, independent of their topical similarity. We then present TEMPS (Temporal Embedding Model for Precise Search), a modular temporal branch that attaches to a frozen semantic retriever and trains on that signal. It resolves anchored temporal expressions to intervals and moment-matches each one to a Gaussian; the resulting ordering supervises an anchor-date-conditioned encoder, whose score we fuse with the semantic score at inference. Grounding supplies the supervision, so training uses no hand-labeled temporal data. The temporal score itself is the Gaussian-KL inclusion measure from distributional embeddings; what TEMPS adds is the grounding and the moment-matched supervision. On three temporal benchmarks, TEMPS improves MRR for every semantic backbone tested and, on TS- Retriever, lifts R@1 from 19.92 to 25.39 over the prior temporal state of the art.

</details>


### [56] [A Native-Reference Coordinate Geometry for L2 Pronunciation Deviation Using Self-Supervised Speech Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28060)
*Tina Raissi,Nhan Phan,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"母语参考坐标几何"方法，用母语语音的phone-class均值构建低维参考子空间，通过L2语音到匹配母语phone-class坐标的距离来做发音评估，无需平行录音或标注，距离与口语熟练度呈负相关（Spearman达-0.5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督语音模型编码了丰富的音素信息，但如何将其转化为可解释的指标，用于自发语音中的二语（L2）发音评估尚不清晰。

Method: 在母语语音中，用各phone-class的平均值定义一个低维参考子空间；对L2语音，计算其到匹配的母语phone-class坐标的距离进行评估。与以往基于距离的方法不同，该方法不需要语言内容匹配的平行录音，也不需要专门的发音标注。研究还考察了不同的自监督编码器与建模选择。

Result: 在不同自监督编码器和建模选择下，得到的母语参考距离与口语熟练度呈负Spearman相关，最高约-0.5，即熟练度越高的说话者越接近母语参考空间。

Conclusion: 母语参考坐标几何可将自监督语音模型的音素信息转化为可解释的L2发音评估指标，且无需平行数据或发音标注，距离能反映熟练度。

Abstract: Self-supervised speech models encode rich phonetic information, but it remains unclear how to transform this information into interpretable metrics for second-language (L2) pronunciation assessment in spontaneous speech. We propose a native-reference coordinate geometry in which phone-class averages from native speech define a low-dimensional reference subspace, and L2 speech is evaluated by its distance to matching native phone-class coordinates. Unlike prior distance-based approaches, our method does not require parallel recordings with matched linguistic content or dedicated pronunciation labels. Across different self-supervised encoders and modeling choices, the resulting native-reference distances show negative Spearman correlations up to -0.5 with speaking proficiency, indicating that higher-proficiency speakers tend to lie closer to the native-reference space.

</details>


### [57] [Reference-Based Analysis of Coherence and Diversity in Open-Ended Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28080)
*Esteban Garcés Arias*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个基于参考的框架，从三个视角（与人类轨迹对齐、与人类续写摘要比较、在人类参考分布下的似然）分析连贯性和多样性，并考察其与人类质量评分的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式文本生成的质量评估需要理解续写的不同属性（连贯性、多样性）如何与其感知质量相关联，需要结构化方法来区分"与人类参考的相似度"和"质量本身"。

Method: 基于参考的框架，从三个视角考察连贯性与多样性：(1) 将其演化与人类轨迹对齐；(2) 与同一提示的人类续写摘要进行比较；(3) 估计其在人类参考分布下的似然。

Result: 基于多样性的对齐和均值比较能捕捉与质量相关的变异，但比较未显示时序对齐相较简单基线的预测优势；参考似然与评分正相关，结果因参考配置和评分范围而异。

Conclusion: 该分析提供了一种结构化方式来考察连贯性与多样性如何与人类判断相关，同时区分了与人类参考的相似度和质量本身。

Abstract: Evaluating open-ended text generation involves understanding how different properties of a continuation relate to its perceived quality. We present a reference-based framework for examining coherence and diversity through three perspectives: aligning their evolution with human trajectories, comparing their summaries with a human continuation of the same prompt, and estimating their likelihood under a human reference distribution. Experiments with human quality ratings suggest that diversity-based alignment and mean-based comparisons capture quality-related variation, although the comparisons do not establish a predictive advantage for temporal alignment over simpler baselines. Reference likelihood also shows positive associations with ratings, with results varying across reference configurations and scoring horizons. Together, these analyses provide a structured way to examine how measured coherence and diversity relate to human judgments, while distinguishing similarity to human references from quality itself. Code and analysis resources are available at https://github.com/EstebanGarces/likely_human.

</details>


### [58] [Scaling Attention Head Analysis via Gradient-Based Attribution in Context-Aware Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28117)
*Paweł Mąka,Yusuf Can Semerci,Jan Scholtes,Gerasimos Spanakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于梯度的注意力头归因方法，将Token级Max-Margin损失反向传播到注意力图，对LLM注意力头进行大规模因果分析，并在上下文感知机器翻译的消歧任务上取得发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要对LLM的注意力头进行大规模因果分析，但现有方法难以适用于大模型，因此需要一个可扩展的框架来研究注意力头功能。

Method: 提出基于梯度的头归因策略，将Token级Max-Margin损失反向传播至注意力图，用于对注意力头进行因果分析。在上下文感知机器翻译的消歧任务上评估，覆盖4个模型、4个语言方向的50种现象。

Result: 在3个模型、2个语言方向上验证了该方法与提高token间关系注意力分数效果的一致性，证明其鲁棒性。分析发现存在"通用型"注意力头，关注不同关系时均能提升模型性能；同时发现注意力头对某关系的平均注意力并不必然与模型性能相关。

Conclusion: 模型在训练中可能形成了注意力头功能上的冗余，平均注意力无法作为头重要性的可靠指标。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a gradient-based head attribution strategy where the Token-level Max-Margin loss is backpropagated to the attention maps. This framework enables a large-scale causal analysis of attention heads, making it suitable for LLMs. We evaluate our method on the task of disambiguation in Context-aware Machine Translation, where we analyze 50 phenomena across 4 models and 4 language directions. We empirically show the alignment of our method with the effects of increasing the attention scores of token-to-token relations on three models and two language directions, ensuring the robustness of our method. Our analysis reveals the presence of the "general-purpose" attention heads that improve the model's performance when attending to different relations. We find that the average attention a head assigns to a relation does not necessarily relate to the model's performance, which suggests that the models developed redundancies during training in terms of the head functions.

</details>


### [59] [Exact Feedback Is Not Control: Evaluating Text-based Closed-Loop Revision in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28150)
*Haitong Jiang,Chunlin Liu,Yile Wang,Yuhong Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出固定预算的修订协议，使用确定性验证器报告所有剩余违规，从而隔离模型侧修订行为，发现在精确反馈下闭环修订仍不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环修订在LLM应用中日益普及，但失败原因可能来自反馈不完整或对正确反馈响应不力，需分离这两种因素。

Method: 引入固定预算修订协议，用确定性验证器报告精确长度、词汇和组合约束下的所有剩余违规，固定反馈正确性与完整性以隔离模型行为；在19个开源和闭源模型上进行对照实验与匹配状态干预。

Result: 控制器层平均最终联合成功率为17.4%至99.8%，相同初始草稿下仍存在显著跨模型差距；失败轨迹常重复早期输出，先前复现与后续可恢复性降低相关；匹配状态干预可改变复现逃逸但不稳定提升最终成功率。

Conclusion: 精确反馈使修订错误可观测，但并不能使闭环修订变得可靠。

Abstract: Closed-loop revision is increasingly used in large language model (LLM) applications, but failures may reflect incomplete feedback or ineffective responses to correct feedback. We introduce a fixed-budget revision protocol with deterministic verifiers that report all remaining violations across exact-length, lexical, and compositional constraints. Fixing feedback correctness and completeness isolates model-side revision behavior. Across 19 open- and closed-source models, controller-level mean final joint success ranges from 17.4% to 99.8%, with substantial cross-model gaps persisting under identical initial drafts. Controlled experiments reveal reproducible model-specific responses to exact feedback. Post-training and scale reshape these responses without consistently bringing them closer to exact correction. Across all constraint families, failed trajectories often repeat earlier outputs, and prior recurrence is associated with lower subsequent recoverability. Matched-state interventions show that removing earlier dialogue while holding the current draft and feedback fixed changes recurrence escape without reliably improving final success; effects depend on the model, task, and trigger-state composition. Exact feedback makes revision errors observable, but does not make the closed loop reliable. Code and reproduction instructions: https://github.com/kevinjiang0121-cyber/exact-feedback-code.

</details>


### [60] [Beyond Poetry: Can Large Language Models Generate Classical Arabic Maqamat?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28245)
*AbdulRahman A. Morsy,Aya Zirikly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM生成玛卡梅（maqama）这一古典阿拉伯韵文散文体裁进行受控评估，比较5个模型在零样本、少样本和规则提示下的表现，发现提示策略显著影响风格质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在创意文本生成上表现强劲，但其生成具有文化根基和风格约束的古典文学形式的能力尚未充分探索。以往研究多聚焦现代语言变体和诗歌，玛卡梅等古典散文传统几乎未被研究。玛卡梅以韵文（saj）、密集修辞和片段式叙事为特征，是检验LLM能否超越表层流畅、达到深层文学能力的理想测试平台。

Method: 首次对LLM玛卡梅生成进行受控评估：比较5个模型在零样本、少样本和基于规则三种提示策略下的输出；通过人工标注和LLM-as-a-judge框架，从修辞丰富度、saj密度、结构连贯性和风格真实性等维度评估；并辅以第二个独立LLM评判、配对统计显著性检验及非LLM的saj代理指标加以验证。

Result: 提示策略对风格质量影响显著：少样本提示最稳定地提升saj密度，但对修辞和连贯性的效果因模型而异；最强模型（GPT-4o和GPT-5.4-mini）在这些维度上从规则提示中获益最多；然而零样本提示在全部5个模型上取得最高综合得分。此外，各模型在与阿拉伯玛卡梅传统的风格契合度上存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统评估了LLM生成玛卡梅的能力，揭示了提示策略、模型差异与文化风格对齐之间的关系，为LLM在古典文学体裁中的文化根基能力提供了新的实证洞见。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance in creative text generation, yet their ability to produce culturally grounded and stylistically constrained literary forms remains underexplored. Prior work has focused largely on modern language varieties and poetry, while classical prose traditions such as maqama remain largely unstudied. The maqama is a classical literary genre characterized by rhymed prose (saj), dense rhetorical ornamentation, and episodic narrative structure, making it a challenging testbed for evaluating whether LLMs can move beyond surface fluency toward deeper literary competence. In this paper, we present the first controlled evaluation study of maqama generation with LLMs, comparing five models under zero-shot, few-shot, and rule-based prompting, and evaluating outputs through both human annotation and an LLM-as-a-judge framework across dimensions such as rhetorical richness, saj density, structural coherence, and stylistic authenticity. Our results show that prompting strategy plays a strong role in stylistic quality: few-shot prompting most consistently improves saj density, while its effects on rhetoric and coherence vary by model, with the strongest models (GPT-4o and GPT-5.4-mini) benefiting most from rule-based prompting on these dimensions, though zero-shot prompting yields the highest aggregate scores across all five models. We further observe systematic differences between models in stylistic alignment with Arabic maqama conventions, and corroborate our findings with a second independent LLM judge, paired statistical significance testing, and non-LLM proxy measures of saj.

</details>


### [61] [Complementary Roles of Activation and Parametric Memory in Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28250)
*Miaohe Niu,Runsong Zhao,Xinyu Liu,Bo Jin,Yucheng Qiao,Chunliang Zhang,Jingbo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究LLM中激活记忆(KV缓存)与参数记忆在少样本学习中的相互作用，发现二者各有所长且需协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活记忆与参数记忆在LLM测试时的作用及其相互关系尚不明确，尤其缺乏对两者interplay的系统性探究。

Method: 通过受控实验系统研究两类记忆在少样本学习中的作用，并进行神经元级分析，考察模型访问相同历史信息时激活的神经元集合。

Result: 激活记忆在事实回忆上更优；参数记忆在任务学习上并不一致地超越激活记忆；复合任务Conditional Arithmetic需两者协同。两类记忆激活不同神经元集合，结合时模型会同时招募两组神经元。

Conclusion: 单独任一记忆机制对复合任务都不充分，二者协作对求解复合任务至关重要。

Abstract: At test time, large language models (LLMs) can encode historical information in activation memory (i.e., KV caches) and parametric memory (i.e., updated parameters). While activation memory is generally considered effective for factual recall and parametric memory for learning new tasks, their interplay remains unclear. In this work, we systematically investigate the role of memory in few-shot learning through controlled experiments. We find that activation memory is superior for recalling facts, whereas parametric memory does not consistently outperform activation memory in task learning. Moreover, our experiments show that the composite task, Conditional Arithmetic, requires the synergy of both memory types. Through neuron-level analysis, we find that the model activates distinct sets of neurons when accessing the same historical information through activation versus parametric memory. When both memory types are combined, the model recruits neurons from both sets, which is crucial for solving Conditional Arithmetic. These findings suggest that neither memory mechanism alone is sufficient for this composite task, highlighting the importance of their collaboration.

</details>


### [62] [Predicting Quantization Price for Selecting PTQ Configurations Before Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28270)
*Junbin Qiu,Jian Mu,Weitong Zhang,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将权重空间训练后量化(PTQ)建模为部署前的配置选择问题，用"定价后的层输出误差"统一评估有限格式、粒度、量化器族、变换和比特数的候选配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法虽能预测重构误差、Hessian敏感度、变换效果和下游损失等退化要素，但通常先固定量化几何再评分，或局限于单一配置族内比较，无法在部署前整体选择最优配置。论文希望打破这种割裂，将配置选择问题统一化。

Method: 将每个可容许的层配置视为一个带部署成本的误差生成器，诱导出层输出误差协方差Σ_l(α_l)；全精度模型用下游曲率对该协方差"定价"，得到 ρ̂_l(α_l)=½Tr(Ĥ_l Σ̂_l(α_l))。该价格来自全精度到量化的前向KL，在参考模型处一阶项抵消。通过迹约简得到校准时的价格表和预算受限的价格引导选择器，重构分数与对角分数成为丢弃价格因子的简化代理。

Result: 该方法使有限格式、码本、粒度和等价变换通过其诱导的协方差与成本成为可比较的候选；固定几何下的比特分配成为该框架的特例，而非组织性问题本身。

Conclusion: 论文提出以定价层输出误差为核心的统一PTQ配置选择框架，将多种退化要素与配置维度纳入同一比较标准，并将传统比特分配问题归约为特殊情形。

Abstract: Weight-space post-training quantization (PTQ) must choose finite formats, granularities, quantizer families, transformations, and bits before the completed quantized model reveals its output-distribution drift. Existing PTQ methods predict important pieces of this degradation, including reconstruction error, Hessian sensitivity, transformation effects, and downstream loss, but these pieces are usually scored after fixing the quantization geometry or inside separate configuration families. We formulate weight-space PTQ as pre-deployment configuration selection using priced layer-output error. Each admissible layer configuration is treated as an error generator with a deployment cost, which induces a layer-output error covariance $\boldsymbolΣ_l(α_l)$, and the full-precision model prices that covariance by downstream curvature, $\widehatρ_l(α_l)=\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_l\,\widehat{\boldsymbolΣ}_l(α_l)\right)$. The price follows from full-precision-to-quantized forward KL, whose first-order term cancels at the reference model. It turns reconstruction and diagonal scores into reduced proxies that drop price factors, while finite formats, codebooks, granularities, and equivalent transformations become comparable candidates through the covariances they induce and the costs they pay. A trace reduction then yields a calibration-time price table and a budgeted price-guided selector, making fixed-geometry bit allocation a special case rather than the organizing problem.

</details>


### [63] [Computation Over Geometry: Meaning Identity Is Computed, Not Shipped in the Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28290)
*Jiaqi Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 语义同一性（paraphrase identity）并非独立编码句向量的几何属性，而是两句在同一前向传播中共同计算出的算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索与RAG通常把"两句是否同义"当作独立编码句向量之间的几何事实（如余弦相似度），作者质疑这一假设，探究identity是否真的存在于off-the-shelf编码器的嵌入几何中。

Method: 在overlap-matched PAWS-X上用探针比较两类设定：独立编码（BGE/E5/GTE/MiniLM/E5-Mistral等句编码器；Llama 3/Mistral/Qwen的last-token状态；晚融合）与联合前向传播（两句话一次forward）。并跨架构验证（因果LM、双向编码器DeBERTa/RoBERTa、编码器-解码器Flan-T5/T5/BART），做partner shuffle、深度、规模、蒸馏等分析。

Result: 独立编码英文confirm AUC仅0.55-0.65（稠密峰值0.70），晚融合接近随机；联合前向传播达0.90-0.96（1.5B-32B），shuffle后崩塌，3B时饱和约0.94，GPT-2 XL为0.76。线性reader无法解锁identity，非线性pair reader仅在49k全训练集上部分恢复（0.68-0.87）。reranker分化：BGE-reranker-large 0.94，MS-MARCO与Jina 0.55-0.64。1.5B联合reader可从无标注teacher分数蒸馏出该关系。

Conclusion: Cosine只比较措辞邻域；identity是廉价的、被计算出的算子，不是任一句向量的属性。bi-encoder可微调拟合PAWS（0.87-0.93）但迁移与STS-B下降。

Abstract: Meaning identity (whether two sentences say the same thing after wording changes) is treated in retrieval and RAG as a geometric fact about independently encoded sentence vectors. We show that, for frozen off-the-shelf encoders and language models, it is not: identity is computed when both sentences share one forward pass, and is not a property of the embedding geometry those systems ship. On overlap-matched PAWS-X, purpose-built encoders (BGE, E5, GTE, MiniLM, E5-Mistral-7B) reach English confirm AUC only 0.55-0.65 (dense peak 0.70). Independently encoded last-token states of Llama 3, Mistral, and Qwen do no better; late fusion of the two vectors stays near chance. The same probe on a joint forward pass reaches 0.90-0.96 from 1.5B to 32B, collapses under partner shuffle, is mid-depth, saturates near 0.94 by 3B, and appears more weakly in GPT-2 XL (0.76). The gap holds beyond Llama-style models on other causal LMs, bidirectional encoders (DeBERTa, RoBERTa), and encoder-decoders (Flan-T5, T5, BART). Fixed or linear readers over frozen independent encodings never unlock identity; nonlinear pair readers recover part of it only on the full 49k-pair PAWS train split (0.68-0.87). Off-the-shelf rerankers split: BGE-reranker-large reaches 0.94, while MS-MARCO and Jina stay at 0.55-0.64. Independently trained families compute the same relation and a 1.5B joint reader can distill it from unlabelled teacher scores, while no linear function of the teachers own independent vectors can. Bi-encoders can be fine-tuned to fit PAWS (0.87-0.93), but transfer and STS-B suffer. Cosine compares wording neighbourhoods; identity is a cheap computed operator, not a property of either sentence vector.

</details>


### [64] [Fine-Tuning LLMs for Translation: General Forgetting Mitigation Does Not Preserve MT-Specific Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28395)
*Niklas Scholz,David Thulke,Abdallah Nasir,Will Allred,Evgeny Matusov,Hermann Ney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究微调大语言模型进行机器翻译时，通用能力的灾难性遗忘及方法缓解效果，是否可迁移到MT指令跟随任务（如正式度、语法性别、长度控制）。比较三类缓解方法，发现EWC最能保留通用能力，但对MT-IF控制任务效果有限，仅数据混合能保留控制但对新提示泛化差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调LLM于平行数据可提升翻译质量但导致灾难性遗忘，现有缓解方法多以通用基准上的保持度来评估。作者质疑这些结论是否能迁移到机器翻译微调及MT特定的指令跟随（MT-IF），如修改译文正式度、语法性别和长度控制，这方面的评估尚不充分。

Method: 先以Llama 3.2 1B Instruct进行筛选研究，再在Llama 3.1 8B Instruct上用双向阿拉伯语-英语或西班牙语-英语数据微调。比较三类缓解方法：依赖辅助数据、依赖模型输出、依赖基础模型参数，并以通用基准保持度与MT-IF控制任务表现为指标。

Result: EWC在两个阶段都最能保留通用能力：8B西班牙语模型上通用基准平均分仅下降1.7分，而标准微调下降11.0分；但其正式度与语法性别控制得分接近标准微调。只有数据混合（加入控制任务样本）能保留这些控制能力，然而其增益无法迁移到同一任务的新提示上。

Conclusion: 通用基准上的遗忘缓解结论不能简单迁移到MT-IF。EWC利于保留通用能力但无助于MT-IF控制；数据混合虽能保留控制但不泛化。MT-IF的遗忘缓解需要专门评估与针对性方法。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models on parallel data improves translation quality but can cause catastrophic forgetting. Mitigation methods are generally evaluated by retention on general benchmarks. We ask whether these findings transfer to machine translation (MT) fine-tuning and to MT-specific instruction following (MT-IF): instructions that modify a translation, such as formality, grammatical gender, and length control. We compare methods anchored to auxiliary data, to model outputs, and to the base model parameters, first in a screening study with Llama 3.2 1B Instruct, then on Llama 3.1 8B Instruct fine-tuned on bidirectional Arabic-English or Spanish-English data. Elastic Weight Consolidation preserves general capabilities best in both stages; on the 8B Spanish model the average score on general benchmarks drops 1.7 points versus 11.0 for standard fine-tuning, yet its scores for formality and grammatical gender control remain close to standard fine-tuning. Only data mixing with control-task examples preserves these controls, but its gains do not transfer to unseen prompts for the same task.

</details>


### [65] [Agent-Editing World Model: Rethinking World Modeling for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28416)
*Shuang Sun,Guoxin Chen,Fanzhe Meng,Jia Deng,Huatong Song,Jinhao Jiang,Wayne Xin Zhao,Hongteng Xu,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Agent-Editing World Model (AEWM)，不再模拟工具响应，而是建模推理与动作如何塑造未来任务进展，缓解任务状态污染并提升智能体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言世界模型预测环境观测，但重建高熵、依赖执行的工具响应在真实反馈可用时价值有限；同时智能体面临任务状态污染，历史中不支持的假设和过时计划会扭曲后续决策。

Method: AEWM 结合 Action Judge 区分 Critical、Exploratory、Noisy 决策，并用 State Revision 对同一观测历史中的噪声推理-动作续写进行编辑；EditAct 将这些能力与真实执行整合，直接改变后续决策的状态；通过 mid-training 和 SFT 在 Search、Terminal、Software Engineering 领域训练。

Result: Action Judge 基准上达到 70.5% macro-F1，超最强前沿基线 10.6 分；六个基准、三种智能体骨干上，EditAct 平均分较最强基线提升 3.2–6.7 分；AEWM-RFT 在三个领域较 Self-RFT 提升 2.2–2.6 分。

Conclusion: 建模任务进展并主动编辑状态比模拟工具响应更有效，AEWM/EditAct 显著改善智能体决策，AEWM-RFT 无需在线指导即可迁移收益。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled agents to tackle long-horizon tasks across diverse environments. To further improve agent performance, existing language world models typically predict environment observations, yet reconstructing high-entropy, execution-dependent tool responses offers limited value when real feedback is available. Meanwhile, agents suffer from \emph{task-state contamination}, where unsupported assumptions and outdated plans persist in history and distort subsequent decisions. We propose the \textbf{Agent-Editing World Model (AEWM)}, which models how reasoning and actions shape future task progress rather than simulating tool responses. AEWM combines \textbf{Action Judge} to distinguish \textsc{Critical}, \textsc{Exploratory}, and \textsc{Noisy} decisions with \textbf{State Revision} to edit noisy reasoning--action continuations from the same observed history. \textbf{EditAct} integrates these capabilities with real execution, directly changing the state underlying subsequent decisions rather than merely providing critiques. We train AEWM across Search, Terminal, and Software Engineering through mid-training and supervised fine-tuning. AEWM achieves 70.5\% macro-F1 on our Action Judge benchmark, exceeding the strongest frontier baseline by 10.6 points. Across six benchmarks and three agent backbones, EditAct improves average scores by 3.2--6.7 points over the strongest baseline. Furthermore, rejection sampling fine-tuning on verified EditAct trajectories, termed \textbf{AEWM-RFT}, improves over Self-RFT by 2.2--2.6 points across three domains without online AEWM guidance.

</details>


### [66] [Cross-Scale Transfer Learning for Depression Severity Prediction: From PHQ-8 to HAMD-17 Across Languages and Clinical Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28430)
*Wenjie Feng,Sahba Zojaji,Satoshi Nakamura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出序列LoRA跨尺度迁移方案，从英文DAIC-WOZ迁移到中文PDCH，在数据稀缺下预测抑郁严重度得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床访谈文本的抑郁严重度连续预测面临数据稀缺问题，单一目标数据集样本不足，需要利用跨语言、跨量表的迁移来提升小样本下的性能。

Method: 采用序列低秩适配（LoRA）协议：Qwen3主干加有界回归头，先在英文DAIC-WOZ（189个虚拟人访谈，PHQ-8）上微调，其适配器再初始化中文PDCH（100个真实临床问诊，HAMD-17）的微调，并重初始化尺度专属回归头预测临床评分。全部配置使用患者级分层5折、2次重复交叉验证。

Result: 在数据稀缺的HAMD-17目标上，序列协议在0.6B和1.7B主干上取得最佳点估计MAE、RMSE和macro-F1，优于仅目标训练和非LLM基线：Qwen3-0.6B为4.96/6.59/0.36，Qwen3-1.7B为4.38/5.62/0.46。消融显示正确对齐的源监督点估计最佳（无监督暴露与打乱标签对照也有部分增益），原生中文输入优于机器翻译英文输入，逆序迁移在运行间方差内无明显收益。

Conclusion: 该研究属探索性单中心内部评估，未确立筛查或诊断效用，也无法单独区分量表、语言或范式偏移的贡献；据作者所知，此前无研究评估该特定DAIC-WOZ到PDCH序列迁移设置。

Abstract: This work addresses continuous depression-severity score prediction from clinical interview transcripts under data scarcity. We propose a sequential low-rank adaptation (LoRA) protocol for cross-scale transfer: a Qwen3 backbone with a bounded regression head is first fine-tuned on the English DAIC-WOZ dataset (189 avatar-mediated sessions, PHQ-8), and the adapter then initializes fine-tuning on the Chinese PDCH dataset (100 real clinical consultations, HAMD-17), where a reinitialised, scale-specific head predicts the clinician-assigned score. All configurations use patient-level stratified 5-fold, 2-repeat cross-validation. On the data-scarce HAMD-17 target, the sequential protocol attains the best point-estimate MAE , RMSE, and macro-$F_1$ on both 0.6B and 1.7B backbones, outperforming target-only training and non-LLM baselines---4.96/6.59/0.36 with Qwen3-0.6B and 4.38/5.62/0.46 with Qwen3-1.7B. Ablations suggest that correctly aligned source supervision gives the best point estimates (unsupervised exposure and shuffled-label controls also show partial gains), that native-Chinese target input outperforms machine-translated English input, and that the reversed order yields no clear gain within run-to-run variance. The study is an exploratory, single-site internal evaluation: it does not establish screening or diagnostic utility, nor separately identify the contribution of the scale, language, or paradigm shifts. To our knowledge, no prior study evaluates this specific DAIC-WOZ-to-PDCH sequential transfer setting.

</details>


### [67] [Contrastive Learning for Authorship Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28471)
*Peter Kirby*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了对比学习与分类方法在作者验证任务上的表现，发现对比学习更优，并据此开发了在PAN21上达到98.4%准确率的ModernBERT双编码器模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在作者验证任务中，对比学习方法是否优于传统的基于分类的方法，并识别影响模型性能的关键因素。

Method: 采用对比学习方法，并与基于分类的方法进行对比实验；分析损失函数、批大小、训练时长、预训练模型、输入上下文长度及随机文本片段数据增强等因素的影响；基于此构建ModernBERT双编码器模型。

Result: 在测试设置下，对比学习优于分类方法。所开发的ModernBERT双编码器模型在PAN21作者验证任务上取得98.4%的准确率。

Conclusion: 对比学习是作者验证的有效方法，合理配置关键超参数与数据增强可显著提升性能，最终模型在PAN21任务上表现优异。

Abstract: Our results show that contrastive learning outperforms a classification-based approach to authorship verification under the tested settings. We identify loss function, batch size, training duration, pre-trained model, input context length, and random text span data augmentation as important factors of model performance. Based on these considerations, we develop a ModernBERT Bi-Encoder model that achieves 98.4% accuracy on the PAN21 authorship verification task.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [68] [When Post-Processing Fairness Constraints Help and When They Harm: Evidence from Eight Cross-Domain Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26955)
*Nithin Raghava Ramachandra Narla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FAPE四阶段框架，跨八个领域评估后处理干预(ThresholdOptimizer)，发现单次部署审计不可靠，主张基于基线差异的筛查与持续监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的公平性审计通常只在部署时、单领域上进行，但重训练和用户群变化会使公平性漂移，且单一数据集验证的干预难以泛化到组织部署的异构领域。

Method: FAPE四阶段框架，在八个领域(刑事司法、收入预测、法律录取、信贷、农业贷款、多领域基准语料、医疗、教育)上评估Fairlearn的ThresholdOptimizer后处理干预。指标包括人口统计平价、均衡几率差、差异影响比和精度成本。另用最小群组规模和留出数据拟合阈值两种测量检查，并部署CUSUM监控进行模拟漂移测试。

Result: 干预效果与基线差异大小相关：14个高差异案例中9个改善、4个近公平案例中3个反而恶化。五个高差异例外案例均可在两种测量检查之一逆转为改善。CUSUM监控能区分从未达0.1平价约定与先达标后回退的约束模型。

Conclusion: 单次部署时审计不可靠，应进行基线差异筛查并持续监控公平性。

Abstract: Fairness audits in production ML typically occur once, at deployment, on a single domain. Both fail in practice: fairness can shift after retraining or a changing user base, and interventions validated on one dataset are rarely tested across the heterogeneous domains an organization deploys. We present FAPE (Fairness Auditing for Production Environments), a four-stage framework evaluating a single post-processing intervention, Fairlearn's ThresholdOptimizer, across eight domain evaluations: criminal justice, income prediction, legal admissions, credit lending, agricultural lending, a multi-domain benchmark corpus, healthcare, and education. Each is scored on demographic parity and equalized odds difference, plus disparate impact ratio and accuracy cost where computable. Intervention effectiveness tracks baseline disparity magnitude: across model-domain pairs the constraint improved disparity in 9 of 14 high-disparity cases and worsened it in 3 of 4 near-fair ones. Each of the five high-disparity exceptions reverses under one of two measurement checks, a minimum group size or thresholds fit on held-out data. A CUSUM monitor started at deployment, tested on a simulated shift, separates constrained models that never met a 0.1 parity convention from those that met it and later regressed. A single deployment-time audit is therefore an unreliable guide, which argues for baseline-disparity screening and continuous monitoring

</details>


### [69] [An open benchmark for machine learning-based polymer property prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27036)
*Robert W. Learsch,Nicholas Liesen,Daniel S. Levine,Anna M. Hiszpanski,Evan R. Antoniuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyBench26：一个含约25万数据点、8种性质、涵盖均聚物及交替/无规/嵌段共聚物的开放聚合物性质预测基准，含四项评测任务，比较语言模型、图模型与描述符方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物性质预测缺乏开放、标准化的基准，现有资源仅覆盖均聚物等狭窄的聚合物结构范围，难以严格比较机器学习方法。

Method: 构建包含近25万条聚合物-性质数据点、覆盖8种物理性质的数据集，数据来源包括实验测量、DFT和分子动力学；设计四项评测任务：分布内性质预测、数据集规模缩放、重复单元复杂度、向留出聚合物结构的迁移；对比语言模型、图模型和描述符方法。

Result: 图模型在性质预测中误差最低，在所评估的各训练集规模下保持优势，且对重复单元复杂度增加保持稳健。

Conclusion: PolyBench26为面向日益复杂聚合物设计空间的可复现模型开发提供基础，已开源。

Abstract: Polymer property prediction lacks open, standardized benchmarks that enable rigorous comparison of machine-learning methods, with existing resources covering only a narrow fraction of polymer architectures, such as homopolymers. We introduce Polymer Benchmark 2026 (PolyBench26), an open dataset comprising nearly 250,000 polymer-property datapoints across eight physical properties, including data from experimental measurements, density functional theory, and molecular dynamics. The benchmark supports four evaluation tasks across homopolymers and alternating, random, and block copolymers: in-distribution property prediction, dataset-size scaling, repeat-unit complexity, and transfer to held-out polymer architectures. We compare language model, graph-based, and descriptor-based approaches and find graph-based models provide the lowest errors in property prediction, retain their advantage across the evaluated training-set sizes, and remain robust to increasing repeat-unit complexity. PolyBench26 provides a reproducible foundation for developing models for the increasingly complex polymer design space. The PolyBench26 benchmark is available open-source at https://github.com/rlearsch/PolymerBenchmark2026.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [70] [Propose, Don't Judge: An Anytime-Valid Referee for LLM Agents That Mine Investment Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27051)
*Bo Qu,Mingguang Chen,Licheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"受治理的自我进化"：语言模型智能体负责提出因子，冻结的统计裁判负责评判。实验表明裁判决定假发现数量，提出者决定产出，认证代价是时间延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体已接管量化因子研究全流程（提出、回测、筛选、淘汰），需要明确哪些环节应保留给智能体、哪些应交由不可篡改的程序，以避免自我评判导致的假发现失控。

Method: 设计冻结统计裁判，仅依据提交后揭示的市场结果、以博彩方式对候选因子打分，保证对任意提出策略在任意停时均具假发现控制。在三类提出者（脚本、赌博机、语言模型）与冻结裁判及三个刻意泄密的裁判之间交叉对比，测试环境包括带植入真值的合成世界、探针撰写环境和 CSI 500 十年滚动前推。

Result: 冻结裁判在脚本提出者下比泄密裁判少接纳 5-11 倍的次阈值因子，且无提出者能弥合该差距；语言模型提出者优于脚本、与赌博机持平，并独有自写诊断探针的能力；被接纳的真实因子需等待约 500 个交易日，认证组合的夏普比率因此落后于未把关组合。

Conclusion: 评判属于程序，提出与工具制造属于智能体。

Abstract: Language-model agents now run the whole of quantitative factor research: they propose investment factors, backtest them, select the survivors and retire them. We ask which of those jobs an agent should keep. Our answer is governed self-evolution: the agent may propose, and a frozen statistical referee that the agent cannot touch must judge. The referee scores each candidate only on market outcomes revealed after submission, by betting, so its false-discovery guarantee holds at every stopping time for any proposal policy. We cross three proposers (a script, a bandit and a language model) with this referee and with three deliberately leaky ones, in a synthetic world with planted truth, a probe-authoring environment and a ten-year walk-forward on the CSI 500. Who judges sets the number of false admissions: the frozen referee admits 5-11 times fewer sub-threshold factors than the leaky referees under a scripted proposer, and no proposer closes that gap. Who proposes sets the yield: the language model beats the script, matches the bandit, and adds the one capability a bandit lacks, writing its own diagnostic probes. The certificate's price is time: an admitted true factor waits about 500 trading days, and the certified portfolio's Sharpe ratio therefore trails an ungated one. Judging belongs to the procedure; proposing and instrument-making belong to the agent.

</details>


### [71] [Silent Failures in Agent-Tool Interaction: An Audit of ToolUniverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26836)
*Shreya Gopalan,Devansh Singh,Sundaraparipurnan Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出并审计"静默失败"——即生物学Agent调用工具时表面成功但信息/功能缺失且无任何通知的失败。通过审计机制、7个失败位点和91个失败样本分析，发现大多数静默失败源于API层(51)和wrapper层(25)并向下游传播，进而提出"上下文可靠性"概念及检测、披露、监控、度量的机制建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究关注Agent系统的任务成功率与完成度，但对Agent与工具交互的失败研究有限，尤其在生物学Agent工作流中更是如此。现有讨论忽视了一类关键失败：工具调用表面成功，实际信息/功能不完整或缺失，且无任何通知，导致用户或Agent对失败毫不知情。

Method: 构建审计机制识别Agent-工具交互中的静默失败；围绕7个失败位点刻画失败发生位置；在ToolUniverse环境中检查15个科学工具及其API与工具文档；采用LLM候选发现、人工验证与自动化测试相结合的方式验证失败。

Result: 共确认91个静默失败，最常见类型为数据或字段缺失，以及搜索、过滤、排名标准不一致。多数失败集中在API层(51)和wrapper层(25)，并可能在下游被放大。静默失败源于上游事件，却传播至看似有效的科学输出中。

Conclusion: 静默失败具有源头在上游、影响传播到下游的特点，会污染科学输出。为应对该问题，论文提出"上下文可靠性"概念，并建议在Agent-工具交互流水线中引入测试、披露、监控与度量此类失败的机制。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are increasingly adopting automated pipelines that integrate multiple tools. While prior research and benchmarks have studied about task success and task completion of these agentic systems, the research about agent to tool interaction, specifically in biology agentic workflow is limited. This study investigates specific failures in agent to tool interaction where a tool invocation appears successful, some or all of the information or functionality from the tool via API/ wrapper is incomplete or missing and there are no communications / notifications to the user or the agent about such missing information. We call this a silent failures as the user or the agents are not aware that such failure has occurred. For the purposes of this study we developed an audit mechanism to identify such silent failures in Agent to tool interaction, by examining 15 scientific tools (and their associated API documentation and tool documentations) integrated within ToolUniverse environment (ToolUniverse serves as our experimental environment rather than the object of the study itself). We structure our study around 7 failure locus characterising where the failure occurs in the chain. We observed 91 failures (manually validated post LLM based candidate discovery and automated testing), most frequent of them being missing data or fields and inconsistencies in search, filtering or ranking criteria. Most of the 91 failures occurred in API layer (51) or wrapper layer (25), with a potential of silent failure amplification downstream. The results show that silent failures originate upstream of the event and propagate downstream into apparently valid scientific outputs. We propose a concept of contextual reliability to handle such failures and suggest mechanisms for testing, disclosing, monitoring, and measuring such failures across the agent-tool interaction pipeline.

</details>


### [72] [Harness as a Language: A Minimalist Agent Framework With Maximal Expressivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26891)
*Zhening Li,Joshua Liu,Mateja Vukelic,Nicole Shen,Supriya Lall,Amitayush Thakur,Alex Zhang,Omar Khattab,Jonathan Light,Armando Solar-Lezama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出JAZ框架，其核心是一个极简的LLM原语invoke，能在无外部记忆/自改进系统的情况下完成长时程记忆与持续自改进任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型智能体围绕agent loop构建，但记忆系统、自改进系统等仍需额外工程。作者想探索一个仅比agent loop稍多的极简harness能达到何种程度。

Method: JAZ暴露单一LLM原语invoke，并内置hooks用于约束与监控。invoke是最简单的、满足两个性质的loop：(1) LLM可写含递归invoke的任意可执行代码；(2) LLM可见的一切输入与交互历史都是代码环境中的变量。将invoke视为一种语言原语，其实现由LLM在每次调用时运行时提供。

Result: 仅用prompting、无手工设计工具或外部系统时：在StuLife需超上下文记忆的长时程部分，invoke比Letta(MemGPT)高8%且成本减半；在AppWorld持续自改进上，invoke比ACE高4%且成本更低。

Conclusion: 一个极简的、仅比agent loop稍多的harness即可复现专门系统所针对的工作流，验证了invoke原语设计的有效性。

Abstract: Modern language-model agents are built around the \textit{agent loop}, where the LLM is placed in an environment exposing a set of tools, and the LLM has full control over the workflow by alternating between tool calls and observing their output. However, certain workflows currently require additional engineering beyond the agent loop itself, such as memory systems and self-improving systems. We built an LLM agent framework, JAZ, to explore the extent to which a minimal harness that is little more than the agent loop itself can accomplish tasks these specialized systems are built for. JAZ exposes a single LLM-based primitive invoke and provides a set of built-in hooks that allow the programmer to apply constraints and monitoring. Generalizing existing code-mode agent loops, \texttt{invoke} is the simplest loop that satisfies two defining properties: (1) the LLM can write arbitrary executable code that can include recursive \texttt{invoke}; (2) everything visible to the LLM --- all inputs to \texttt{invoke} as well as its interaction history with the code environment --- are variables in the code environment. We motivate our design from first principles, viewing \texttt{invoke} as a language primitive representing a function whose implementation is provided at runtime by an LLM every time it is called. To validate the design of our core \texttt{invoke} primitive, we evaluate \texttt{invoke} --- with only prompting, no manually designed tools, harness, or external systems (e.g., memory or the file system) --- on workflows traditionally implemented through specialized external harnesses. On long-horizon workflows requiring recall beyond the context window, JAZ invoke outperforms Letta (MemGPT) by 8\% at half its cost on the recall-heavy portion of StuLife. On continual self-improvement, JAZ invoke outperforms ACE by 4\% at a lower cost on AppWorld.

</details>


### [73] [TwinCheck: Evidence-Grounded Negative-Twin Verification for Stateful Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26911)
*Jiaxuan Dai,Tianyi Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TwinCheck是一种推理时验证策略，仅在轨迹满足证据条件时考虑替换agent的动作，通过构造"负孪生"候选并做双向偏好验证，在BFCL V4任务上将成功率从45.3%提升到58.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个局部看似合理的工具调用就可能毁掉原本成功的agent轨迹，但仅凭怀疑不足以进行干预——因为替换本身可能引入验证本想防止的失败。

Method: 提出TwinCheck：当轨迹满足与局部失败假设相关的证据条件时，才考虑替换；构造轨迹grounded的反事实替代（"负孪生"），仅当孪生通过结构检查且成对验证器在两种候选顺序下都偏好它时才替换。评估采用精确重放，固定agent的解析响应与动作直到首次被接受的替换。

Result: 在159个有多轮完整精确重放对（complete exact-replay pairs）的BFCL V4任务上，完整策略将GPT-5.6 Sol的任务成功率从45.3%提升至58.5%（95%任务bootstrap CI [8.2, 18.8]），未观察到成功转失败的回归。

Conclusion: 将执行边界的修复重新定义为一种受约束的比较，使反事实动作本身成为验证对象。

Abstract: A single locally plausible tool call can derail an otherwise successful agent trajectory. Suspicion alone does not justify intervention, because the replacement itself can introduce the very failure verification is meant to prevent. We introduce TwinCheck, an inference-time verification policy that considers replacement only when the trace satisfies an evidence condition tied to a trace-local failure hypothesis. It constructs a trace-grounded counterfactual alternative, a negative twin, and replaces the agent's proposal only if the twin passes structural checks and the pairwise verifier prefers it in both candidate orders. For paired evaluation, exact replay holds the agent's parsed responses and actions fixed until the first accepted replacement, separating intervention effects from resampling. In the primary analysis of 159 multi-turn BFCL V4 tasks with complete exact-replay pairs, the complete policy raises task success for GPT-5.6 Sol from 45.3% to 58.5% (95% task-bootstrap CI [8.2, 18.8]), with no observed success-to-failure regressions. Together, these findings recast execution-boundary repair as a constrained comparison, making the counterfactual action itself the object of verification.

</details>


### [74] [Building Socio-Affective Artificial Intelligence for Interactive Multi-Agent Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26927)
*David Berga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AGIMUD软件架构，为人类与多个智能体在模拟动态世界中的交互提供设计原则，融合社会感知推理、情感、多模态交互方案与分布式AI处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前通用人工智能（AGI）时代，基于Transformer的对话智能体激增且算力提升，但缺乏整合性的智能体交互设计，尤其是智能体与自身及人类的交互。为实现未来人-智能体推理系统的可持续性与治理，需要明确的多智能体心智理论（社会与情感感知）设计。

Method: 提出软件AGIMUD，集成三部分：（A）智能体行为与交互中的社会感知推理及情感；（B）面向人类用户、人工智能体与模拟世界的多模态交互方案；（C）通过网络分布式AI处理以支持多个自主智能体。据此将动态世界重建为多用户地牢（MUDs）。

Result: 实现了AGIMUD系统，允许多个智能体与人类在同一动态世界中实时交互；代码已开源于GitHub（https://github.com/dberga/AGIMUD）。

Conclusion: 整合社会感知、情感与分布式多智能体交互的架构，对于构建可持续且可治理的未来人-智能体推理系统至关重要，AGIMUD为此提供了可用的设计范式与实现。

Abstract: The objective of this article is to provide design principles and a software architecture for enabling interaction between humans and multiple agents in simulated dynamic worlds. This connects the current era of general artificial intelligence (AI/AGI) with the proliferation of transformer-based conversational agents and the increased computational capabilities. Given an overview of current and previous multi-agent theories of mind (socially and affectively-aware agents), the existence of an integrative design of agent interactions with themselves and with humans must be crucial for understanding how to create sustainable and governance in future human-agent reasoning systems.
